__ test_precached_helper_imports_offline_in_a_fresh_page_and_panels_stay_lazy __
[gw3] linux -- Python 3.13.18 /usr/local/bin/python3

    @pytest.mark.skipif(shutil.which("node") is None, reason="node binary not on PATH")
    def test_precached_helper_imports_offline_in_a_fresh_page_and_panels_stay_lazy():
        script = r'''
          import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
          import { chromium } from 'playwright';
          const origin = process.env.ODYSSEUS_TEST_STATIC_ORIGIN;
          const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
          try {
            const context = await browser.newContext({ serviceWorkers: 'allow' });
            const page = await context.newPage();
            await page.goto(`${origin}/static/js/panels.js`);
            const installed = await page.evaluate(async () => {
              await navigator.serviceWorker.register('/static/sw.js?v=20260916autoscroll1');
              const registration = await navigator.serviceWorker.ready;
              if (!navigator.serviceWorker.controller) await new Promise(resolve => navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener('controllerchange', resolve, { once: true }));
              const response = await caches.match('/static/js/actionMenuOrder.js');
              return { scope: registration.scope, cached: !!response && response.ok };
            });
            assert.equal(installed.scope, `${origin}/static/`);
            assert.equal(installed.cached, true);
            // New page gives a fresh ES-module map. No helper import occurred online.
            await context.setOffline(true);
            const offline = await context.newPage();
            await offline.goto(`${origin}/static/js/panels.js`);
            const requests = [];
            offline.on('request', request => requests.push(request.url()));
            const result = await offline.evaluate(async () => {
              const helper = await import('/static/js/actionMenuOrder.js');
              const again = await import('./actionMenuOrder.js');
              const panels = await import('/static/js/panels.js?v=20260909movepicklayer1');
              return {
                sameModule: helper === again,
                exports: Object.keys(helper).sort(),
                order: helper.orderActionMenuItems([{ label: 'Delete' }, { label: 'Rename' }]).map(i => i.label),
                panels: panels.panelNames(),
              };
            });
            assert.equal(result.sameModule, true);
            assert.deepEqual(result.order, ['Rename', 'Delete']);
            assert.ok(result.panels.includes('editor'));
            assert.equal(requests.some(url => url.includes('/galleryEditor.js')), false);
            console.log(JSON.stringify({ ...result, ...installed }));
          } finally { await browser.close(); }
        '''
        result = subprocess.run(["node", "--input-type=module", "--eval", script], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=90)

_ test_modal_reopen_after_dock_close_and_pending_animation[1440-settings-modal] _
[gw0] linux -- Python 3.13.18 /usr/local/bin/python3

modal_id = 'settings-modal', width = 1440

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('modal_id', ['settings-modal', 'theme-modal'])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize('width', [1440, 390])
    def test_modal_reopen_after_dock_close_and_pending_animation(modal_id, width):
        script = r"""
          import { chromium } from 'playwright';
          const browser = await chromium.launch({headless:true});
          try {
            const page = await browser.newPage({viewport:{width:WIDTH,height:1000}});
            await page.route('**/api/**', route => route.fulfill({json:{value:null}}));
            await page.goto(process.env.ODYSSEUS_TEST_STATIC_ORIGIN+'/static/js/storage.js');
            await page.setContent('__STYLES__<div id="sidebar"></div>');
            const result = await page.evaluate(async id => {
              const html = await (await fetch('/static/index.html')).text();
              const source = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html,'text/html');
              document.body.append(source.getElementById(id));
              const modal = document.getElementById(id);
              const content = modal.querySelector('.modal-content');
              const manager = await import('/static/js/modalManager.js');
              let open, close;
              if(id === 'settings-modal') {
                const lifecycle = await import('/static/js/settings/lifecycle.js');
                open = () => lifecycle.showSettingsModal(modal);
                close = () => lifecycle.hideSettingsModal(modal);
              } else {
                const theme = await import('/static/js/theme.js');
                // The old sidebar simply removed hidden; the public opener now
                // also cancels a pending close and restores manager state.
                open = theme.openPopup || (() => modal.classList.remove('hidden'));
                close = () => theme.closePopup();
              }
              const visible = () => getComputedStyle(modal).display !== 'none';
              const wait = () => new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,350));
              const states = {};
              if (id === 'theme-modal') {
                const theme = await import('/static/js/theme.js');
                theme.togglePopup();
                theme.togglePopup();
                theme.togglePopup();
                await wait();
                states.fastToggleReopens = visible();
                close();
                await wait();
              }
              open();
              manager.register(id,{closeFn:close,restoreFn:()=>{}});
              manager.minimize(id);
              manager.close(id);
              await wait();
              open();
              await wait();
              states.afterDockClose = visible();
              // An opening/descendant animation must not reach an orphan closer.
              content.dispatchEvent(new AnimationEvent('animationend',{bubbles:true,animationName:'modal-enter'}));
              states.afterOpeningAnimation = visible();
              // Begin a visible close, then reopen before its fallback expires.
              close();
              open();
              await wait();
              states.afterFastReopen = visible();
              manager.register(id,{closeFn:close,restoreFn:()=>{}});
              manager.minimize(id);
              open();
              states.directOpenRestoresMinimized = visible() && !manager.isMinimized(id);
              // A child animation must not terminate a normal parent close.
              close();
              content.querySelector('button').dispatchEvent(new AnimationEvent('animationend',{bubbles:true}));
              states.childAnimationDoesNotClose = !modal.classList.contains('hidden');
              content.dispatchEvent(new AnimationEvent('animationend',{bubbles:true,animationName:'modal-exit'}));
              states.normalCloseCompletes = modal.classList.contains('hidden') && !content.classList.contains('modal-closing');
              // Completing by animation must also remove the timer.
              open();
              await wait();
              states.reopenAfterCompletedAnimation = visible();
              close();
              await wait();
              states.fallbackCompletes = modal.classList.contains('hidden') && !content.classList.contains('modal-closing');
              return states;
            }, MODAL_ID);
            console.log(JSON.stringify(result));
          } finally { await browser.close(); }
        """
        script = script.replace('WIDTH', str(width)).replace('MODAL_ID', json.dumps(modal_id))
        script = script.replace('__STYLES__', stylesheet_link_tags())
        result = subprocess.run(['node','--input-type=module','-e',script], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
>       assert result.returncode == 0, result.stdout + result.stderr
E       AssertionError: node:internal/modules/run_main:123
E             triggerUncaughtException(
E             ^
E         
E         Error [ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND]: Cannot find package 'playwright' imported from /home/user/odysseus/[eval1]
E         Did you mean to import "playwright/index.js"?
E             at Object.getPackageJSONURL (node:internal/modules/package_json_reader:314:9)
E             at packageResolve (node:internal/modules/esm/resolve:772:81)

E             at defaultResolve (node:internal/modules/esm/resolve:989:11)

              if (id === 'theme-modal') {
                const theme = await import('/static/js/theme.js');
                theme.togglePopup();
                theme.togglePopup();
                theme.togglePopup();
                await wait();
                states.fastToggleReopens = visible();
                close();
                await wait();
              }
              open();
              manager.register(id,{closeFn:close,restoreFn:()=>{}});
              manager.minimize(id);
              manager.close(id);
              await wait();
              open();
              await wait();
              states.afterDockClose = visible();
              // An opening/descendant animation must not reach an orphan closer.
              content.dispatchEvent(new AnimationEvent('animationend',{bubbles:true,animationName:'modal-enter'}));
              states.afterOpeningAnimation = visible();
              // Begin a visible close, then reopen before its fallback expires.
              close();
              open();
              await wait();
              states.afterFastReopen = visible();
              manager.register(id,{closeFn:close,restoreFn:()=>{}});
              manager.minimize(id);
              open();
              states.directOpenRestoresMinimized = visible() && !manager.isMinimized(id);
              // A child animation must not terminate a normal parent close.
              close();
              content.querySelector('button').dispatchEvent(new AnimationEvent('animationend',{bubbles:true}));
              states.childAnimationDoesNotClose = !modal.classList.contains('hidden');
              content.dispatchEvent(new AnimationEvent('animationend',{bubbles:true,animationName:'modal-exit'}));
              states.normalCloseCompletes = modal.classList.contains('hidden') && !content.classList.contains('modal-closing');
              // Completing by animation must also remove the timer.
              open();
              await wait();
              states.reopenAfterCompletedAnimation = visible();
              close();
              await wait();
              states.fallbackCompletes = modal.classList.contains('hidden') && !content.classList.contains('modal-closing');
              return states;
            }, MODAL_ID);
            console.log(JSON.stringify(result));
          } finally { await browser.close(); }
        """
        script = script.replace('WIDTH', str(width)).replace('MODAL_ID', json.dumps(modal_id))
        script = script.replace('__STYLES__', stylesheet_link_tags())
        result = subprocess.run(['node','--input-type=module','-e',script], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('modal_id', ['settings-modal', 'theme-modal'])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize('width', [1440, 390])
    def test_modal_reopen_after_dock_close_and_pending_animation(modal_id, width):
        script = r"""
          import { chromium } from 'playwright';
          const browser = await chromium.launch({headless:true});
          try {
            const page = await browser.newPage({viewport:{width:WIDTH,height:1000}});
            await page.route('**/api/**', route => route.fulfill({json:{value:null}}));
            await page.goto(process.env.ODYSSEUS_TEST_STATIC_ORIGIN+'/static/js/storage.js');
            await page.setContent('__STYLES__<div id="sidebar"></div>');
            const result = await page.evaluate(async id => {
              const html = await (await fetch('/static/index.html')).text();
              const source = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html,'text/html');
              document.body.append(source.getElementById(id));
              const modal = document.getElementById(id);
              const content = modal.querySelector('.modal-content');
              const manager = await import('/static/js/modalManager.js');
              let open, close;
              if(id === 'settings-modal') {
                const lifecycle = await import('/static/js/settings/lifecycle.js');
                open = () => lifecycle.showSettingsModal(modal);
                close = () => lifecycle.hideSettingsModal(modal);
              } else {
                const theme = await import('/static/js/theme.js');
                // The old sidebar simply removed hidden; the public opener now
                // also cancels a pending close and restores manager state.
                open = theme.openPopup || (() => modal.classList.remove('hidden'));
                close = () => theme.closePopup();
              }
              const visible = () => getComputedStyle(modal).display !== 'none';
              const wait = () => new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,350));
              const states = {};
              if (id === 'theme-modal') {
                const theme = await import('/static/js/theme.js');
                theme.togglePopup();
                theme.togglePopup();
                theme.togglePopup();
                await wait();
                states.fastToggleReopens = visible();
                close();
                await wait();
              }
              open();
              manager.register(id,{closeFn:close,restoreFn:()=>{}});
              manager.minimize(id);
              manager.close(id);
              await wait();
              open();
              await wait();
              states.afterDockClose = visible();
              // An opening/descendant animation must not reach an orphan closer.
              content.dispatchEvent(new AnimationEvent('animationend',{bubbles:true,animationName:'modal-enter'}));
              states.afterOpeningAnimation = visible();
              // Begin a visible close, then reopen before its fallback expires.
              close();
              open();
              await wait();
              states.afterFastReopen = visible();
              manager.register(id,{closeFn:close,restoreFn:()=>{}});
              manager.minimize(id);
              open();
              states.directOpenRestoresMinimized = visible() && !manager.isMinimized(id);
              // A child animation must not terminate a normal parent close.
              close();
              content.querySelector('button').dispatchEvent(new AnimationEvent('animationend',{bubbles:true}));
              states.childAnimationDoesNotClose = !modal.classList.contains('hidden');
              content.dispatchEvent(new AnimationEvent('animationend',{bubbles:true,animationName:'modal-exit'}));
              states.normalCloseCompletes = modal.classList.contains('hidden') && !content.classList.contains('modal-closing');
              // Completing by animation must also remove the timer.
              open();
              await wait();
              states.reopenAfterCompletedAnimation = visible();
              close();
              await wait();
              states.fallbackCompletes = modal.classList.contains('hidden') && !content.classList.contains('modal-closing');
              return states;
            }, MODAL_ID);
            console.log(JSON.stringify(result));
          } finally { await browser.close(); }
        """
        script = script.replace('WIDTH', str(width)).replace('MODAL_ID', json.dumps(modal_id))
        script = script.replace('__STYLES__', stylesheet_link_tags())
        result = subprocess.run(['node','--input-type=module','-e',script], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)

    def test_modal_visibility_is_available_offline_before_first_open():
        script = r"""
          import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
          import { chromium } from 'playwright';
          const browser = await chromium.launch({headless:true});
          try {
            const context = await browser.newContext({serviceWorkers:'allow'});
            const page = await context.newPage();
            const origin = process.env.ODYSSEUS_TEST_STATIC_ORIGIN;
            await page.goto(origin+'/static/js/storage.js');
            await page.evaluate(async () => {
              await navigator.serviceWorker.register('/static/sw.js');
              await navigator.serviceWorker.ready;
              if(!navigator.serviceWorker.controller) await new Promise(resolve =>
                navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener('controllerchange',resolve,{once:true}));
              const response = await caches.match('/static/js/modalVisibility.js');
              if(!response?.ok) throw new Error('Modal helper is missing from install-time precache');
            });
            await context.setOffline(true);
            const offline = await context.newPage();
            await offline.goto(origin+'/static/js/storage.js');
            const exports = await offline.evaluate(async () =>
              Object.keys(await import('/static/js/modalVisibility.js')).sort());
            assert.deepEqual(exports,['hideModal','showModal']);
          } finally { await browser.close(); }
        """
        result = subprocess.run(['node','--input-type=module','-e',script], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=90)

owner = 'bg:chat-1'

    def acquire(spec: ContainmentSpec, *, owner: str) -> ContainmentGrant:
        """Establish containment, or refuse.
    
        Postcondition under :data:`MODE_ENFORCING`, asserted rather than assumed::
    
            spec.required <= grant.enforced
    
        Picks the strongest available mechanism and never substitutes a weaker one
        for a required dimension. Under :data:`MODE_REPORT_ONLY` the same shortfall
        lands in ``grant.unenforced_required`` and is logged, so the run is
        distinguishable from a contained one after the fact.
    
        :raises ValueError: the spec is malformed (a caller bug, in either mode).
        :raises ContainmentUnavailable: a required dimension is unavailable, under
            :data:`MODE_ENFORCING`.
        """
        owner_id = str(owner or "").strip()
        if not owner_id:
            # A process with no owner is a process nothing will reap.
            raise ValueError("containment: every grant needs an owner")
        spec = _validate_spec(spec)
    
        mechanism, provided = _select(spec)
        enforced = provided & spec.requested
        missing_required = frozenset(spec.required) - enforced
        name = mechanism.name if mechanism else "none"
    
        if missing_required and CONTAINMENT_MODE == MODE_ENFORCING:
            # The command does not run. This is the whole point: "not executed" is
            # the one outcome a model cannot mistake for success.
>           raise ContainmentUnavailable(missing_required, name)
E           src.containment.ContainmentUnavailable: containment unavailable (filesystem, process_tree); strongest mechanism available was 'process_group'
